| HD-476 | 2 | AI | 1 | **Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B as the laptop's vision leg — the runtime's only resident generation leg since the 2026-10-09 ruling (FIM is the other one; one GPU, one leg resident).** Split out of HD-474 on 2026-09-30 evidence. Gemma 4 26B A4B **kills the runtime** on any image whose long side exceeds ~1120 px (engine exit 3221226505 = STATUS_STACK_BUFFER_OVERRUN; server answers `{"error":"terminated"}`). Reproduced on TWO weight files — lmstudio-community Q4_K_M and unsloth QAT UD-Q4_K_XL — and on both a 1280x960 photo and a 2000x160 strip, so it is **not** the quant, the file size, or total pixels: it is **max side length** (320 / 640 / 896 / 1120 px all answer; 1280 and 2000 both die). Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct (unsloth UD-Q4_K_XL 17.72 GB + mmproj-BF16 1.09 GB) takes every one of them **and the untouched 8160x6120 rack photo** (prompt_tokens 4060, survived, engine still IDLE) and reads rack text (`CAT6`, the 1-24 port numbering, `Cloud Router Switch`, a `CRS3xx` model). **It does NOT transcribe, and that corrected this row:** asked for the switch model number five times (3 at the file's sampling, 2 greedy at `temperature 0`) it answered `CRS326-24G-2S+RM` every time, while [network-rack.md](docs/network-rack.md) U15 records `CRS328-24P-4S+` behind a label photo; greedy agreement rules out sampling noise, so the 2026-09-29 "OCR'd it verbatim" result is **not reproducible** and the label photo, not the model, stays the authority (`raw/91`, `raw/92`) Measured here: decode **23.84 t/s** (beats Gemma's 17.5), image prefill **61.2 t/s** (66 s for one full-res photo), tool call well-formed, `-c 32768 --parallel 1` = **23.86 GiB** of the 24 GiB adapter (KV is **96 KiB/token** — 48 layers x 4 kv heads x 128, no sliding window — i.e. **5x Gemma's ~20 KiB/token**); the model trains at 262144, the box cannot reach it. 📦 **Landed 2026-09-30 (overnight, `session/hd474-win11-certify-2`; committed by `d261c15f`).** Two profiles then, ONE leg now: the text-only agent profile this row was split against left the catalogue with the 2026-10-09 ruling, so the loud `400 "does not support image inputs"` that used to be the proof a text row cannot take an image is no longer reachable from the picker. What stands is `vision-qwen3vl-30b` — the dial's ACTIVE profile, a 32k **f16 KV** profile at **23.51 of 32 GiB** declared, booting in 28 s at **23.12 GiB** measured, `certified: true` from `raw/88-certify.txt`. Image cost re-measured: 4042–4060 tokens / **61–72 s** full resolution, 937 tokens / 13.6 s at 1120 px — plus the cache rule that comes with it: **the identical question again costs 2.0 s, any new question re-pays the ~60 s**, because the text precedes the image in the message, so a seam that asks one photo several things must put the image first or freeze the question. Two file-level traps stand: LM Studio's downloader auto-pulls **mmproj-F32 (2.29 GB)** and `lms get <file-url>` fetches the whole repo — always fetch the exact two files with `huggingface_hub` `allow_patterns`; and unsloth's own commands use **mmproj-BF16**, never F32. 📦 **The SKU question closed the same evening** (`raw/96`): the photo was cut into a 2x3 grid chosen without looking at it, and a quarter of the SAME frame read `CRS328-24P-4S-RM` four times while five of the other tiles answered `NOT VISIBLE` (no invented SKU) and the repo's own label photo `docs/assets/images/CRS328.png` came back **`CRS328-24P-4S+RM` character-perfect, `+` included, for 528 tokens in 3.9 s**. So the wide-frame misread was **pixel allocation, not sight**, and image tokens **saturate** (~4.1 k for the full frame and for a 3264x3672 quarter alike; 1120 px = 937; label closeup = 528) — **crop, do not shrink**; the whole frame at one fifth could not see the label at all. Greedy agreement had proven stability, not perception, and that was the trap. ⏳ **Still unmeasured — and since 2026-10-09 this is the ONLY number this leg owes:** the 23.51 GiB declared budget has never been exercised with an image actually resident in the KV of a 32k window — the number is header arithmetic plus a text-only 32k load (`raw/ctx32k.out`), and those ~4 k image tokens land in the same KV. Take it against the loaded leg with `probe-vision --image <the 8160x6120 rack photo> --expect CAT6` — an `--expect` the WHOLE frame supplies reliably, which the part number below is not — and read the PDH dedicated-memory counter while it prefills · [reports/hd474-laptop-leg/](reports/hd474-laptop-leg/) · [hardware-workstation.md](docs/hardware-workstation.md) §Served leg · 📋 [`prompt-laptop-legs.md`](prompt-laptop-legs.md) |

## 3b. Deploy path — Ansible-only (HD-150, supersedes HD-02 Doco-CD)

> **Doco-CD is DROPPED (HD-150).** Deployment + upgrades use **Ansible only** for BOTH VPS and
> oldsrv. The single upgrade path: **Renovate** opens Forgejo PRs → the **Forgejo Actions deploy
> button** (`workflow_dispatch`) runs Ansible (idempotent `docker_compose_v2` re-render/applies the
> merged image-tag) → post-deploy renders Homepage config + inventory. Homepage is a dashboard link to
> the Dependency Dashboard + deploy button (no deploy role). Old HD-02 activation notes removed.

## 3c. Deploy-gated verification — lives in `deployment-tasks.md`

> **Per-phase `Deploy-gated verification` checklists are the authoritative home** for every `⏳` (IaC-done, not-live-verified) row, ordered by the deployment phase (1/1.5/2/3/4/6/8) where each live-verify runs.
> See [`deployment-tasks.md`](deployment-tasks.md) → each phase's `Deploy-gated verification` block. Row detail stays in the owning doc. This section is now a pointer, not a second SSOT.

| Ansible | roles/templates/conventions | `docs/deployment-ansible.md`, `IaC/README.md`

| Validation | `bash scripts/validate-all.sh` before commit | `scripts/`

## 6. Service onboarding

> Uniform 10-step path for adding a service (exposure/auth → secrets → compose → registry → edge →
> state/backup → observability → validation → deploy gate → docs). Canonical copy:
> **`CONVENTIONS.md` §5 — Service-onboarding checklist**. Reproduced there; new services must clear all 10 steps.
